问题与改进

每条按等级与类型标注，展开可看触发条件、实际观察、验收标准、证据原文与可直接粘贴的上游 Issue 草稿。评审发现不等于本轮实际阻塞，也不因业务通过而标为已解决。

独立评测发现

来源：独立评审 · 口径 v2 · 源 Run 37879768670 / attempt 1

最新上游状态：未复核。原评审“已解决”不作为上游修复证明；应用绕行、业务 QA 通过均不关闭框架问题。

等级与类型怎么定
  • 严重阻塞交付，或造成数据丢失、安全问题，且没有合理绕行
  • 重要按指引或公开 API 正常使用会静默得到错误结果，或只能用非公开手段绕行
  • 轻微能完成，但要多花排查成本或在应用侧绕行
  • 信息措辞、示例完整度等不影响结果的改进
  • 实现缺陷实现违背文档或公开约定
  • 能力缺口需求属于框架职责，但缺少能力或公开接入
  • 指引错误指引、示例或注释写错，或彼此矛盾，照做会出错
  • 指引缺失指引缺少必要说明或示例，需要读源码或试探
  • 易用性改进现有能力可用，但有改进空间

NocoBase3 库、插件、模板与指引 4

重要能力缺口上游未复核反馈复核：证据支持原评审置信度：confirmed
F3 · 缺少公开的服务级 workflow 版本启用入口，应用须绕过公开契约

NocoBase3 插件 · @nocobase/app-plugin-workflow · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-workflow/SKILL.md · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-workflow/references/invocation-and-service-api.md · 原评审：未解决

⌄

反馈证据核对

六项核对后结论成立。contract：server 公开入口只导出 workflowServiceToken，其类型 WorkflowServiceContract 仅有 registerInstruction/trigger；internalWorkflowServiceToken 与 WorkflowService 的 discoverArtifacts/ensureArtifactMaterialized 均不在公开导出，SKILL 也要求只用公开导出、不得绕经内部或物化表。behavior：应用 provider 实际把该 token 结构 cast 为 WorkflowActivationRuntime，调用物化方法后直接 update workflows 表把 revision 标为 current/enabled。application：公开契约无服务级启用入口，应用无法装配，只能如此绕行；管理 HTTP enable(hash) 虽存在但要 manage 权限，不是服务级/启动装配入口。environment：B02 定向复测通过，说明并非环境或业务失败。factory：SKILL 把 enablement 划给管理 API，却未给出启动激活的受支持装配方式。existing-capability：HTTP/CLI 管理面与 WorkflowService 内部物化方法存在，但没有公开服务级激活 API，故为真实能力缺口；建议属能力改进而非违约。

  • 职责与公开约定：公开 server 入口导出 workflowServiceToken（WorkflowServiceContract 仅 registerInstruction/trigger），internalWorkflowServiceToken 及服务物化方法未导出。 E1 E2 E3 E5
  • 实际行为证据：WorkflowService 实现含 discoverArtifacts/ensureArtifactMaterialized 但非公开；应用 provider 以结构 cast 调用它们并直接写 workflows 表 current/enabled。 E3 E6
  • 应用接入与业务边界：应用无公开服务级启用入口可用，只能结构 cast workflowServiceToken 并直接更新物化表，属被契约限制的装配方式。 E6
  • 环境因素：定向复测 B02 passed、无控制台/页面错误，排除外部服务或运行环境造成该现象的解释。 E7
  • 工厂因素：工厂 Skill 要求用公开 API 且把版本 enablement 划给管理 API，但未提供启动时激活已部署 revision 的受支持装配方式。 E4 E5
  • 已有能力与推荐用法：管理 HTTP 有 enable(idOrArtifactHash)/POST /api/workflows/{hash}/enable，但需 manage 权限，属管理路由而非服务级 API；WorkflowService 的物化方法未公开。 E87 E3 E5

反馈复核是对原始反馈的证据核对，不等于人工确认、上游修复状态或自动修复授权；保留原评审置信度、分类与处理状态。

公开 server 入口只给 workflowServiceToken=[REDACTED] registerInstruction 与 trigger；能物化/激活版本的 WorkflowService 由未导出的 internalWorkflowServiceToken 引用（E1/E2/E3）。SKILL 明令“use public package exports and APIs; do not bypass them through plugin internals or materialized tables”，并把 enablement 划给管理 API（E4/E5）。应用为让随源码部署的 workflow 安装即可运行，只能自定义 WorkflowActivationRuntime 结构 cast token 并直接改 workflows 表标记 current/enabled（E6）。

实际影响
应用每次启动都要依赖结构 cast 与直接写物化表；插件内部字段或表结构变化会使激活静默失效，且违背公开/内部边界。
建议改法
在 @nocobase/app-plugin-workflow/server 暴露一个受控的服务级启用 API（例如 enableDeployedRevision(key)/activateArtifact(hash)），或在 Skill 中给出受支持的启动激活装配方式。
诊断 · 触发条件、应有行为、绕行代价与验收标准
触发条件
应用通过 ServiceProvider 在 boot() 中启用随源码构建、已物化但默认 disabled 的 workflow 版本；公开契约没有对应方法。
应有能力 / 行为
框架提供公开、稳定的服务级入口启用已部署 revision，或在指引中给出受支持的装配方式。
实际观察
公开契约只有 registerInstruction/trigger；应用 cast workflowServiceToken 并直接更新 workflows 表把 revision 标记为 current/enabled。
应用绕行与代价
应用自定义 WorkflowActivationRuntime 结构、调用 discoverArtifacts/ensureArtifactMaterialized 并直接写物化表；依赖插件内部布局。
改进后的验收标准
在不 import 插件内部、不写物化表的前提下，新应用能仅用公开 API 让已部署 workflow 在启动后处于 enabled 且可 trigger；回归时删除应用侧 cast 与表写入仍通过 B02。
评审时证据 · 冻结快照 5 条
packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-workflow/dist/server/tokens.d.ts:4–9E1

WorkflowServiceContract 只暴露 registerInstruction 与 trigger；internalWorkflowServiceToken 单独声明且未随公开契约导出。

export interface WorkflowServiceContract {
    registerInstruction(instruction: WorkflowInstructionClass): void;
    trigger(workflowKey: string, input: JsonObject, options?: WorkflowEventOptions): Promise<WorkflowTriggerReceipt>;
}
export declare const workflowServiceToken=[REDACTED] declare const internalWorkflowServiceToken=[REDACTED]
packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-workflow/dist/server/service.js:83–112E3

WorkflowService 提供 discoverArtifacts/restoreClientArtifacts/synchronizeDeploymentArtifacts/ensureArtifactMaterialized 及 assertResourcesPresent，并在版本缺失时提示启用新版本或调 enable 管理接口。

    discoverArtifacts() {
        return this.loader.discover();
    }
    restoreClientArtifacts() {
        return this.loader.restoreClientArtifacts();
    }
    synchronizeDeploymentArtifacts() {
        return this.loader.synchronizeDeploymentArtifacts();
    }
    ensureArtifactMaterialized(hash) {
        return this.loader.ensureMaterialized(hash);
    }
    async dispose() {
        try {
            await this.initializationPromise;
        }
        catch {
            // A failed lazy initialization still leaves engine.dispose() safe to call.
        }
        this.initializationPromise = undefined;
        await this.engine.dispose();
    }
    /** Every materialized version executes its stored resources in all environments. */
    async assertResourcesPresent(workflow) {
        const hash = workflow.hash;
        if (!hash || !(await this.store.has(workflow.key, hash)))
            throw new Error(`Workflow Artifact ${workflow.key}/${String(hash)} is missing from this build. ` +
                'The database still points at the hash of an earlier build, so enable the version this build deployed: ' +
                'choose "Enable new version" on the workflow in workflow management, or call POST /api/workflows/<hash>/enable with the new hash. ' +
                'Enabling by workflow id keeps the missing hash.');
app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-workflow/SKILL.md:19–19E4

“Use public package exports and APIs; do not bypass them through plugin internals or materialized tables.”

The application owns workflow source packages, business services, trigger timing, authentication and authorization, business idempotency, and compensation policy. The plugin owns the DSL and core Instructions, Artifact and execution lifecycle, persisted history, management API, and diagnostic views. Use public package exports and APIs; do not bypass them through plugin internals or materialized tables.
app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-workflow/references/invocation-and-service-api.md:45–50E5

明确公开 server 入口只导出 workflowServiceToken，其公开契约只有 registerInstruction() 与 trigger()，其余为包内方法。

The public server entry exports `workflowServiceToken`. The Workflow
ServiceProvider registers the service in the application's container. Its
public contract exposes `registerInstruction()` for application extensions and
`trigger()` for business invocation; management routes use additional
package-internal methods. Do not bind services to `AppRuntime` or add a mutable
plugin-services object.
app/server/providers/equipment-workflow-provider.ts:9–34E6

应用注释承认 workflowServiceToken 落在更窄的 WorkflowServiceContract 下，需自定义 WorkflowActivationRuntime 结构并 cast 以访问物化能力，再标记 current/enabled。

/**
 * The workflow runtime methods this provider needs. `workflowServiceToken` is
 * published under the narrower `WorkflowServiceContract`; the service object
 * behind it also exposes artifact materialization, which is what activation
 * uses to create the revision row before marking it current and enabled.
 */
interface WorkflowActivationRuntime {
  discoverArtifacts(): Promise<readonly { key: string; digest: string }[]>;
  ensureArtifactMaterialized(digest: string): Promise<number | undefined>;
}

/**
 * Activates the application-owned ticket acceptance workflow.
 *
 * Workflow enablement is deliberately a management decision in the platform:
 * a discovered Artifact is materialized but stays disabled until someone
 * enables it. A business application that ships a source-managed workflow
 * still has to be operable the moment it is installed, so this provider
 * materializes the current Artifact and marks that revision current and
 * enabled. It is idempotent: an already enabled revision is left untouched,
 * and a rebuild that produces a new Artifact hash activates the new revision
 * instead of silently running the old one.
 *
 * This runs on every start, but does nothing when the workflow plugin is
 * absent, so the application still starts without it.
 */
上游 Issue 草稿 · Markdown
### [重要][能力缺口] 缺少公开的服务级 workflow 版本启用入口，应用须绕过公开契约

**相关对象**：`@nocobase/app-plugin-workflow`（评审时 2.0.0-beta.0）、`app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-workflow/SKILL.md`、`app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-workflow/references/invocation-and-service-api.md`

**反馈证据核对**：反馈复核：证据支持

六项核对后结论成立。contract：server 公开入口只导出 workflowServiceToken，其类型 WorkflowServiceContract 仅有 registerInstruction/trigger；internalWorkflowServiceToken 与 WorkflowService 的 discoverArtifacts/ensureArtifactMaterialized 均不在公开导出，SKILL 也要求只用公开导出、不得绕经内部或物化表。behavior：应用 provider 实际把该 token 结构 cast 为 WorkflowActivationRuntime，调用物化方法后直接 update workflows 表把 revision 标为 current/enabled。application：公开契约无服务级启用入口，应用无法装配，只能如此绕行；管理 HTTP enable(hash) 虽存在但要 manage 权限，不是服务级/启动装配入口。environment：B02 定向复测通过，说明并非环境或业务失败。factory：SKILL 把 enablement 划给管理 API，却未给出启动激活的受支持装配方式。existing-capability：HTTP/CLI 管理面与 WorkflowService 内部物化方法存在，但没有公开服务级激活 API，故为真实能力缺口；建议属能力改进而非违约。

- 职责与公开约定：公开 server 入口导出 workflowServiceToken（WorkflowServiceContract 仅 registerInstruction/trigger），internalWorkflowServiceToken 及服务物化方法未导出。；证据：E1（packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-workflow/dist/server/tokens.d.ts:4–9）、E2（packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-workflow/dist/server/index.d.ts:4–6）、E3（packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-workflow/dist/server/service.js:83–112）、E5（app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-workflow/references/invocation-and-service-api.md:45–50）
- 实际行为证据：WorkflowService 实现含 discoverArtifacts/ensureArtifactMaterialized 但非公开；应用 provider 以结构 cast 调用它们并直接写 workflows 表 current/enabled。；证据：E3（packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-workflow/dist/server/service.js:83–112）、E6（app/server/providers/equipment-workflow-provider.ts:9–34）
- 应用接入与业务边界：应用无公开服务级启用入口可用，只能结构 cast workflowServiceToken 并直接更新物化表，属被契约限制的装配方式。；证据：E6（app/server/providers/equipment-workflow-provider.ts:9–34）
- 环境因素：定向复测 B02 passed、无控制台/页面错误，排除外部服务或运行环境造成该现象的解释。；证据：E7（artifacts/verify-7/browser-focused/report.json:1–9）
- 工厂因素：工厂 Skill 要求用公开 API 且把版本 enablement 划给管理 API，但未提供启动时激活已部署 revision 的受支持装配方式。；证据：E4（app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-workflow/SKILL.md:19–19）、E5（app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-workflow/references/invocation-and-service-api.md:45–50）
- 已有能力与推荐用法：管理 HTTP 有 enable(idOrArtifactHash)/POST /api/workflows/{hash}/enable，但需 manage 权限，属管理路由而非服务级 API；WorkflowService 的物化方法未公开。；证据：E87（app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-workflow/references/invocation-and-service-api.md:122–135）、E3（packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-workflow/dist/server/service.js:83–112）、E5（app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-workflow/references/invocation-and-service-api.md:45–50）

反馈复核是对原始反馈的证据核对，不等于人工确认、上游修复状态或自动修复授权；保留原评审置信度、分类与处理状态。

公开 server 入口只给 workflowServiceToken=[REDACTED] registerInstruction 与 trigger；能物化/激活版本的 WorkflowService 由未导出的 internalWorkflowServiceToken 引用（E1/E2/E3）。SKILL 明令“use public package exports and APIs; do not bypass them through plugin internals or materialized tables”，并把 enablement 划给管理 API（E4/E5）。应用为让随源码部署的 workflow 安装即可运行，只能自定义 WorkflowActivationRuntime 结构 cast token 并直接改 workflows 表标记 current/enabled（E6）。

**影响**：应用每次启动都要依赖结构 cast 与直接写物化表；插件内部字段或表结构变化会使激活静默失效，且违背公开/内部边界。

**建议改动**：在 @nocobase/app-plugin-workflow/server 暴露一个受控的服务级启用 API（例如 enableDeployedRevision(key)/activateArtifact(hash)），或在 Skill 中给出受支持的启动激活装配方式。

**验收标准**：在不 import 插件内部、不写物化表的前提下，新应用能仅用公开 API 让已部署 workflow 在启动后处于 enabled 且可 trigger；回归时删除应用侧 cast 与表写入仍通过 B02。

> 来源：nb3-factory 独立搭建评审（口径 v2，源 Run 37879768670 / attempt 1）。尚未复核最新上游源码。
重要能力缺口上游未复核反馈复核：证据支持原评审置信度：confirmed
F4 · Scheduler 缺少公开的按需执行入口，应用反射私有 runner 并直读私有表

NocoBase3 插件 · @nocobase/app-plugin-scheduler · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-scheduler/SKILL.md · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-scheduler/references/operations.md · 原评审：未解决

⌄

反馈证据核对

六项核对后结论成立。contract：schedulerServiceToken 的公开 SchedulerService 只有 registerTarget/defineSchedule，注释与 operations 指引明确 store、target registry、occurrence history 与 executor 私有且不可由容器解析。behavior：Provider 只把 compose().service 注册进容器，executor 与两个 store 是其私有部件，DefaultSchedulerService 持有它们。application：应用重复私有 SCHEDULER_SCOPE、cast schedulerServiceToken、解析 jobExecutorServiceToken.getScheduleExecutor 取得 executor.runner，手写 ScheduleStart/End/Error 并直查 schedule_occurrences，再经路由暴露 runScheduleNow。environment：B08 定向验收 passed，排除环境解释。factory：SKILL 明令不得改 schedule_definitions/schedule_occurrences 或向该 scope 加 job、也不得以容器解析绕过 Routes 授权，却没有公开 run-now 入口，属指引/能力缺口。existing-capability：HTTP 仅有 list/occurrences/enable/disable，CLI 仅有 sync，无按需执行入口，故应用只能触私有 runner。

  • 职责与公开约定：公开 SchedulerService 只承诺 registerTarget 与 defineSchedule；注释与 operations 指引明确 store、registry、occurrence history、executor 私有且不可容器解析。 E16 E21
  • 实际行为证据：Provider 只注册 compose().service，executor 与两个 store 为私有部件；DefaultSchedulerService 构造时持有 store/occurrences/targets。 E17 E18 E19
  • 应用接入与业务边界：应用重复私有 SCHEDULER_SCOPE、cast schedulerServiceToken、反射 executor.runner、手写调度事件并直查 schedule_occurrences，经路由暴露 runScheduleNow。 E22 E23 E24
  • 环境因素：B08 定向验收 passed（待办生成、重复不重复、启停），说明现象不是外部服务或运行环境导致。 E25
  • 工厂因素：工厂 SKILL 禁止改调度表、向 Scheduler scope 加 job 或绕过 Routes 授权，但未提供受支持的按需执行入口与边界。 E20 E21
  • 已有能力与推荐用法：现有公开能力仅为 HTTP list/occurrences/enable/disable 与 CLI sync，均非 run-now；公开服务无执行入口。 E21 E16

反馈复核是对原始反馈的证据核对，不等于人工确认、上游修复状态或自动修复授权；保留原评审置信度、分类与处理状态。

SchedulerService 只公开 registerTarget 与 defineSchedule，注释明确 store、target registry、occurrence history 与 executor 保持私有（E17/E18/E19/E20）；operations 指引进一步说明这些不可通过容器解析绕过 Routes 授权（E22），SKILL 禁止直接改 schedule_definitions/schedule_occurrences 或向 Scheduler scope 添加 job（E21）。但业务验收需要“立即执行一次”，应用只能重复私有 SCHEDULER_SCOPE、cast schedulerServiceToken、解析 jobExecutorServiceToken.getScheduleExecutor 取私有 runner.run，并直查 schedule_occurrences（E23/E24），在路由暴露 runScheduleNow（E25）。

实际影响
按需执行依赖私有字段与私有表：插件重构会让 run-now 失效；同时与 SKILL 的禁止事项直接冲突，Agent 每次都要重新论证绕过。
建议改法
为 Scheduler 提供公开的按需执行 API（如 runNow(scheduleId)）并保留其权限校验；或在 Skill 中给出受支持的运行入口与边界。
诊断 · 触发条件、应有行为、绕行代价与验收标准
触发条件
业务页面需要“立即执行一次”已声明的受控调度，而调度只在 cron/HTTP 管理接口触发。
应有能力 / 行为
框架提供经授权的公开 run-now 入口，避免触碰私有 store/executor。
实际观察
应用重复私有 SCHEDULER_SCOPE、反射取 executor.runner、手写 ScheduleStart/End/Error 并直读 schedule_occurrences。
应用绕行与代价
应用以结构 cast 与私有常量访问 runner，并直接查询私有 occurrence 表；与新版本耦合。
改进后的验收标准
使用公开 API 触发一次调度即可产生正确 occurrence 与业务结果，且应用代码不再出现 SCHEDULER_SCOPE 常量、executor.runner 或直接写 schedule_occurrences。

packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-scheduler/dist/server/tokens.d.ts:4–27E16

SchedulerService 只暴露 registerTarget 与 defineSchedule；注释明确 store、target registry 与 occurrence history 保持私有。

export interface SchedulerService {
    /**
     * Registers what a schedule can point at: how its configuration is
     * validated, how a firing starts, and — for anything that finishes later —
     * how a run is inspected. The returned handle is how that target reports a
     * terminal outcome back.
     */
    registerTarget<TConfig extends JsonObject>(target: ScheduleTargetType<TConfig>): ScheduleTargetHandle;
    /**
     * Registers a scheduled task this app should run. Call during `register()`
     * or `boot()`, the same way as `registerTarget` — schedules are read once
     * when `sync()` runs during `start()`, so anything defined afterward has no
     * effect until the next sync. `key` is the schedule's stable, application-
     * wide identity.
     */
    defineSchedule(definition: ScheduleDefinition): void;
}
/**
 * The scheduler's one public service, carrying its one extension point.
 * Reading and changing schedules is reachable through the plugin's HTTP API
 * and its `scheduler sync` command; the store, the target registry and the
 * occurrence history stay private to this package.
 */
export declare const schedulerServiceToken=[REDACTED]
packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-scheduler/dist/server/providers/scheduler.js:22–27E17

Provider 只把 compose().service 注册为 schedulerServiceToken，registry、executor 与两个 store 是 Provider 自己的部件，不供容器解析。

    register() {
        // One service reaches the container. The target registry, the executor
        // and both stores are this provider's own parts, handed to whatever needs
        // them instead of being resolvable by anyone holding the container.
        this.app.container.singleton(schedulerServiceToken, () => this.compose().service);
    }
packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-scheduler/dist/server/providers/scheduler.js:84–103E18

compose() 解析 jobExecutorServiceToken.getScheduleExecutor(SCHEDULER_SCOPE, jobs) 并以私有 store/occurrences/targets 组装 DefaultSchedulerService。

    compose() {
        if (this.internals)
            return this.internals;
        const container = this.app.container;
        const database = container.resolve(databaseManagerToken);
        const targets = new ScheduleTargetRegistry();
        const occurrences = new ScheduleOccurrenceStore(database);
        // Concurrency and attempts are the selected configuration's. A failed
        // dispatch is recorded as the occurrence's outcome, so a retry of the same
        // firing finds it recorded and does nothing: keep attempts at 1 there.
        const executor = container
            .resolve(jobExecutorServiceToken)
            .getScheduleExecutor(SCHEDULER_SCOPE, this.jobsConfiguration());
        const store = new ScheduleStore(database, this.app.appName, executor, (spec, limit) => createScheduleDispatchJob(spec, limit, { targets, occurrences }));
        this.internals = {
            executor,
            service: new DefaultSchedulerService(store, occurrences, targets),
        };
        return this.internals;
    }
packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-scheduler/dist/server/services/scheduler.js:4–19E19

DefaultSchedulerService 持有 store/occurrences/targets，实现 defineSchedule/registerTarget。

export class DefaultSchedulerService {
    store;
    occurrences;
    targets;
    manifest = [];
    constructor(store, occurrences, targets) {
        this.store = store;
        this.occurrences = occurrences;
        this.targets = targets;
    }
    defineSchedule(definition) {
        this.manifest.push(Object.freeze({ definition: buildScheduleDefinition(definition) }));
    }
    registerTarget(target) {
        this.targets.register(target);
        const { type } = target;
app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-scheduler/SKILL.md:49–49E20

“Do not modify schedule_definitions、schedule_occurrences … and do not add jobs under Scheduler's @nocobase/app-plugin-scheduler scope.”

Schedules are defined in code. There is no management API for creating or editing Cron definitions, but the UI and API support enabling and disabling tasks. Do not modify `schedule_definitions`, `schedule_occurrences`, or the schedule backend's rules (BullMQ job schedulers, or the memory adapter's state file) directly, and do not add jobs under Scheduler's `@nocobase/app-plugin-scheduler` scope.
app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-scheduler/references/operations.md:41–41E21

列表/发生记录/启停只通过 HTTP API，同步通过 CLI；store/registry/executor 私有，应用不能通过容器解析绕过 Routes 授权。

Reading and changing schedules — list, occurrences, enable and disable — is reachable through the HTTP API above, and synchronization through `pnpm nocobase scheduler sync`. Neither is exposed as a resolvable service: the store, the target registry and the schedule executor are private to the plugin, so an application cannot bypass the authorization its Routes enforce by resolving them from the container.
app/server/service/schedule-runner.ts:32–37E22

应用注释承认 SCHEDULER_SCOPE 在插件内未公开导出，因此在应用侧重复该常量，否则 getScheduleExecutor 会返回无处理器的执行器。

/**
 * The scope the plugin registers its executors under. Declared in the plugin's
 * provider but not publicly exported, so it is repeated here; it must match, or
 * `getScheduleExecutor` hands back a different executor with no handlers.
 */
const SCHEDULER_SCOPE = '@nocobase/app-plugin-scheduler';
app/server/service/schedule-runner.ts:144–187E23

应用把 schedulerServiceToken cast 为 ScheduleEventRecorder、解析 jobExecutorServiceToken.getScheduleExecutor 取私有 runner.run，并手写 ScheduleStart/Error/End 事件记录。

  const service = app.container.resolve(
    schedulerServiceToken,
  ) as unknown as ScheduleEventRecorder;
  // The plugin resolves its executor with this same configuration name, and the
  // jobs service caches by (configuration, scope); a different name would build
  // a second executor whose handler table has none of the plugin's schedules.
  const jobsName = app.config.get<string>('scheduler.jobs');
  const executor = app.container
    .resolve(jobExecutorServiceToken)
    .getScheduleExecutor(SCHEDULER_SCOPE, jobsName);
  const runner = (executor as unknown as ExecutorWithRunner).runner;
  if (!runner) {
    return undefined;
  }

  const scheduleId = String(definition.id);
  const now = new Date();
  const occurrenceId = `${scheduleId}:manual:${Date.now()}.${Math.random()
    .toString(36)
    .slice(2, 8)}`;
  // Keep the plan's next timed firing: the store writes `nextRunAt` from every
  // event it records, so omitting it here would clear the schedule's next run.
  const nextRunAt = dateValue(definition.nextRunAt);
  const base = {
    jobId: occurrenceId,
    jobName: scheduleId,
    scheduledAt: now,
    runAt: now,
    ...(nextRunAt ? { nextRunAt } : {}),
  };

  await service.recordEvent({ name: 'ScheduleStart', ...base });
  try {
    await runner.run({ ...base, signal: AbortSignal.timeout(120_000) });
  } catch (error) {
    await service.recordEvent({
      name: 'ScheduleError',
      ...base,
      reason: 'execute-failed',
      error: error instanceof Error ? error : new Error(String(error)),
    });
    throw error;
  }
  await service.recordEvent({ name: 'ScheduleEnd', ...base });
app/server/routes/service-content.ts:403–407E24

业务路由通过 runScheduleNow(app, key) 提供“立即执行”入口。

  const runOperation = async (
    key: string,
    fallback: () => Promise<Record<string, unknown>>,

### [重要][能力缺口] Scheduler 缺少公开的按需执行入口，应用反射私有 runner 并直读私有表

**相关对象**：`@nocobase/app-plugin-scheduler`（评审时 1.0.0-beta.13）、`app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-scheduler/SKILL.md`、`app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-scheduler/references/operations.md`

**反馈证据核对**：反馈复核：证据支持

六项核对后结论成立。contract：schedulerServiceToken 的公开 SchedulerService 只有 registerTarget/defineSchedule，注释与 operations 指引明确 store、target registry、occurrence history 与 executor 私有且不可由容器解析。behavior：Provider 只把 compose().service 注册进容器，executor 与两个 store 是其私有部件，DefaultSchedulerService 持有它们。application：应用重复私有 SCHEDULER_SCOPE、cast schedulerServiceToken、解析 jobExecutorServiceToken.getScheduleExecutor 取得 executor.runner，手写 ScheduleStart/End/Error 并直查 schedule_occurrences，再经路由暴露 runScheduleNow。environment：B08 定向验收 passed，排除环境解释。factory：SKILL 明令不得改 schedule_definitions/schedule_occurrences 或向该 scope 加 job、也不得以容器解析绕过 Routes 授权，却没有公开 run-now 入口，属指引/能力缺口。existing-capability：HTTP 仅有 list/occurrences/enable/disable，CLI 仅有 sync，无按需执行入口，故应用只能触私有 runner。

F5 · authorization 的 server 入口未导出其实现中的 databaseGrant/allScopes/idsScope

NocoBase3 插件 · @nocobase/app-plugin-authorization · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authorization/references/fluent-registration.md · 原评审：未解决

⌄

反馈证据核对

六项核对后结论成立。contract：server/index.d.ts 导出 defineDatabasePermission、DatabasePermissionBuilder、anyScope、condition、scopeAst 等，但未导出实现中存在的 databaseGrant/allScopes/idsScope。behavior：这三个函数在 database/builders.js 与 database/scope.js 中确实实现——databaseGrant 生成 database.collection 许可集合，allScopes 做 and 组合，idsScope 按 id 展开 $eq。application：应用在 service-authorization.ts 内自行重写 databaseGrant 形状与 allOf，并注释与 anyScope 的语义差异。environment：B04 passed，排除环境解释。factory：fluent-registration 只推荐定义式 defineDatabasePermission/defineRecordAccess，未说明与这些未导出 helper 的关系及如何构造 and 作用域。existing-capability：公开的 defineDatabasePermission(...).build() 已能产出 PermissionGrant，databaseGrant 的复写可用公开定义式 API 替代；但 allScopes/idsScope 类 helper 无公开对应，导出面与实现面确不一致，属可改进的能力缺口。

  • 职责与公开约定：server 入口导出 defineDatabasePermission 与 anyScope/condition/scopeAst，但明确未列出 databaseGrant/allScopes/idsScope。 E8 E9 E10
  • 实际行为证据：实现中 databaseGrant 返回 database.collection 许可集合形状；allScopes 做 and 组合、idsScope 按 id 展开 $eq，语义清晰。 E9 E10
  • 应用接入与业务边界：应用在 service-authorization.ts 手写等价 databaseGrant 与 allOf，并注明未公开故自行重写。 E14
  • 环境因素：B04 验收 passed，说明权限组合在运行环境中按预期生效，现象不是环境问题。 E15
  • 工厂因素：fluent-registration 推荐定义式 defineDatabasePermission/defineRecordAccess，但未说明与未导出 databaseGrant/allScopes/idsScope 的关系或替代路径。 E12
  • 已有能力与推荐用法：公开 defineDatabasePermission(...).build() 返回 PermissionGrant 可覆盖集合授权；但 AND 组合与 idsScope 无公开 helper，导出面与实现面仍不一致。 E8 E12

反馈复核是对原始反馈的证据核对，不等于人工确认、上游修复状态或自动修复授权；保留原评审置信度、分类与处理状态。

server/index.d.ts 导出 defineDatabasePermission、DatabasePermissionBuilderDefinition、recordAccess、anyScope、condition、scopeAst（E8），却未导出 databaseGrant、allScopes、idsScope；这些函数在实现中存在且语义清晰（E9/E10）。fluent-registration 指引推荐 defineDatabasePermission 与 defineRecordAccess（E12），应用据此接入，但仍手写 databaseGrant 形状与 allOf（E14）。

实际影响
应用需要复制库内小工具并自担语义（例如 allOf 与 anyScope 的差别），存在把 observerVisible OR not confidential 这类组合写错的风险；导出面与实现面不一致。
建议改法
从 @nocobase/app-plugin-authorization/server 导出 databaseGrant、allScopes、idsScope（或明确标注为内部并仅保留定义式 API），并在 fluent-registration 中说明二者关系。
诊断 · 触发条件、应有行为、绕行代价与验收标准
触发条件
应用按需构造集合授权与 and 组合作用域时，需要与库实现一致的 helper。
应有能力 / 行为
公开 server 入口提供构造 data scope 的 helper，或指引明确只用定义式 API。
实际观察
databaseGrant/allScopes/idsScope 仅在 dist 实现中存在，公开入口未列出，应用手写等价结构。
应用绕行与代价
应用在 service-authorization.ts 内重写 databaseGrant 形状与 allOf，并注释其与 anyScope 的语义差异。
改进后的验收标准
应用可用公开导出构造相同授权而无需自定义 helper；删除应用内 databaseGrant/allOf 后 B04/B05 仍通过。

packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authorization/dist/server/index.d.ts:12–15E8

server 入口导出 DatabasePermissionBuilder/DatabasePermissionDefinitionBuilder/defineDatabasePermission 及 recordAccess、anyScope、condition、scopeAst，未导出 databaseGrant/allScopes/idsScope。

packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authorization/dist/server/database/builders.js:1–11E9

databaseGrant(collection, definition) 在实现中返回 database.collection 许可集合形状，但该函数不在公开 server 入口。

import { ReadPermissionBuilder, WritePermissionBuilder, } from './permission-builders.js';
/** The permission-set grant of one collection. */
export function databaseGrant(collection, definition) {
    return {
        resource: { type: 'database.collection', id: collection },
        actions: Object.entries(definition).map(([action, config]) => ({
            action,
            policy: { type: 'database', ...config },
        })),
    };
}
packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authorization/dist/server/database/scope.js:32–47E10

allScopes/idsScope 在实现中存在（and 组合、按 id 展开 $eq），但未从公开入口导出。

export function allScopes(scopes) {
    if (scopes.includes(false))
        return false;
    const items = scopes.filter((scope) => scope !== true);
    if (items.length === 0)
        return true;
    return items.length === 1 ? items[0] : group('and', items);
}
/**
 * Membership expands to one `$eq` per identifier because `FilterOperator` has
 * no `$in` — `$includes` is a substring test. A negated list would expand the
 * same way, as an `and` of `$ne`. Both collapse once db grows the operator.
 */
export function idsScope(field, ids) {
    return anyScope(ids.map((id) => condition(field, '$eq', id)));
}
app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authorization/references/fluent-registration.md:3–13E12

推荐用定义式 defineDatabasePermission/defineRecordAccess 与 authz.database.collections.add 描述集合权限、数据作用域与记录访问。

Opt collections in with `authz.database.collections.add(...)`; pages need no server registration because the client route tree lists them. Define composite resources with `defineCompositeResource(name, configure)` from `@nocobase/authorization/core` and reusable collection permissions with `defineDatabasePermission(configure)` from `@nocobase/app-plugin-authorization/server`. Both callbacks execute synchronously and return immutable builders; neither registers anything, queries a database or assigns permissions. Register a built resource with `authz.compositeResources.define(resource)`, which returns its `CompositeResourceReference`, and list it in the workspace with `authz.ui.place(reference, { section })`; `resource.reference()` returns the same reference without registering.

Use the complete [quote resource declaration](runtime-api.md#declare-a-business-operation) as the starting point and the [permission-set declaration](code-and-seeds.md#share-declarations-not-runtime-instances) to select its data scopes. This reference adds builder semantics and relation capabilities. A composite can also be written as a plain object, `{ name, title, actions: [{ name, title, dataScopes, grants }] }`, where each data scope is `{ key, title, options?, defaultValue? }` and targets the one resource its grants name; the package README shows both forms.

The row type passed to `.collection<Row>(name)` supplies compile-time field checks, not another runtime schema. DB metadata remains authoritative. Collection registration defines which operations participate in authorization; the permission builder describes requested capabilities independently. The registered Collection action catalogue is enforced when authorizing requests.

`grant(key, permission, { title? })` binds a reusable permission to one data scope of the action. The key is stored as `scopeKey` and names the data scope a grant or rule fills with a record selection. The title defaults to the permission's explicit title, or the collection name; override it when one action uses the same collection twice. Different keys allow independent multi-collection configuration. Repeated keys and operations are rejected. A business action composes only `database.collection` grants.

`read` describes output fields, while `create` and `update` describe input fields. `delete()` takes no fields. Use `'*'` or `.allFields()` explicitly for all fields; for `create` and `update` that means every field a write may set, leaving out auto-increment, generated and optimistic-lock version columns. Never list one of those, or a field that does not exist, in a `create` or `update` list: db refuses the whole Policy, so every write would fail, and the startup scan reports it. `.options(...recordAccessReferences)` limits the record access a grant may choose and keeps the option names typed on the resource reference; `.default(reference)` sets the value used when a grant chooses nothing. The references must apply to the selected collection. Omitting options offers every registered record access that applies.

Define record access with `defineRecordAccess(key, configure)` from `@nocobase/authorization/core`, then register it with `authz.recordAccess.define(access)`. Use `.collections(...names)` for applicability (`'*'` for every collection), `.params<P>(schema)` for typed parameters and `.resolver(fn)` for evaluation. The resolver receives `{ principal, collection, action, params }` and no database objects; it returns `true`, `false`, a filter built with `condition(...)` from `@nocobase/app-plugin-authorization/server`, or a `FilterAst` built with `buildFilter` from `@nocobase/repository-input`, which the database adapter validates and compiles. Grants and rules never store resolver functions: `.options(...)`, `.default(...)`, relation `.recordAccess(...)` and `selection.recordAccess(key, params?)` store keys. Use stable keys because grants and rules persist them.
app/server/service-authorization.ts:67–101E14

应用注释说明 databaseGrant 非公开入口故自行重写形状，allScopes 非公开故手写 allOf，并提示 anyScope 组合观察员范围可能放行保密工单。

/**
 * A collection grant for the database permission type. The library's own
 * `databaseGrant` helper is not part of its public server entry point, so the
 * equally small public shape it builds is written here.
 */
function databaseGrant(
  collection: string,
  definition: Readonly<Record<string, Record<string, unknown>>>,
): PermissionGrant {
  return {
    resource: { type: 'database.collection', id: collection },
    actions: Object.entries(definition).map(([action, config]) => ({
      action,
      policy: { type: 'database', ...config },
    })),
  };
}

function anyOf(field: string, values: readonly unknown[]): DatabaseScope {
  return anyScope(
    values.map((value) => condition(field, '$eq', value as never)),
  );
}

/**
 * Every given condition has to hold. The public server entry point exports
 * `anyScope` (an OR of scopes) and `condition`, but not the library's own
 * `allScopes`, so the `and` group it would build is written here for the one
 * rule that needs two conditions to hold together. Note that an observer
 * scope built with `anyScope` would be `observerVisible OR not confidential`,
 * which lets a confidential ticket through as soon as it is observer-visible.
 */
function allOf(...scopes: readonly DatabaseScope[]): DatabaseScope {
  return { kind: 'group', logic: 'and', items: [...scopes] } as DatabaseScope;
}

### [轻微][能力缺口] authorization 的 server 入口未导出其实现中的 databaseGrant/allScopes/idsScope

**相关对象**：`@nocobase/app-plugin-authorization`（评审时 1.0.0-beta.24）、`app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authorization/references/fluent-registration.md`

**反馈证据核对**：反馈复核：证据支持

六项核对后结论成立。contract：server/index.d.ts 导出 defineDatabasePermission、DatabasePermissionBuilder、anyScope、condition、scopeAst 等，但未导出实现中存在的 databaseGrant/allScopes/idsScope。behavior：这三个函数在 database/builders.js 与 database/scope.js 中确实实现——databaseGrant 生成 database.collection 许可集合，allScopes 做 and 组合，idsScope 按 id 展开 $eq。application：应用在 service-authorization.ts 内自行重写 databaseGrant 形状与 allOf，并注释与 anyScope 的语义差异。environment：B04 passed，排除环境解释。factory：fluent-registration 只推荐定义式 defineDatabasePermission/defineRecordAccess，未说明与这些未导出 helper 的关系及如何构造 and 作用域。existing-capability：公开的 defineDatabasePermission(...).build() 已能产出 PermissionGrant，databaseGrant 的复写可用公开定义式 API 替代；但 allScopes/idsScope 类 helper 无公开对应，导出面与实现面确不一致，属可改进的能力缺口。

- 职责与公开约定：server 入口导出 defineDatabasePermission 与 anyScope/condition/scopeAst，但明确未列出 databaseGrant/allScopes/idsScope。；证据：E8（packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authorization/dist/server/index.d.ts:12–15）、E9（packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authorization/dist/server/database/builders.js:1–11）、E10（packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authorization/dist/server/database/scope.js:32–47）
- 实际行为证据：实现中 databaseGrant 返回 database.collection 许可集合形状；allScopes 做 and 组合、idsScope 按 id 展开 $eq，语义清晰。；证据：E9（packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authorization/dist/server/database/builders.js:1–11）、E10（packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authorization/dist/server/database/scope.js:32–47）
- 应用接入与业务边界：应用在 service-authorization.ts 手写等价 databaseGrant 与 allOf，并注明未公开故自行重写。；证据：E14（app/server/service-authorization.ts:67–101）
- 环境因素：B04 验收 passed，说明权限组合在运行环境中按预期生效，现象不是环境问题。；证据：E15（artifacts/verify-7/browser-acceptance/report.json:85–95）
- 工厂因素：fluent-registration 推荐定义式 defineDatabasePermission/defineRecordAccess，但未说明与未导出 databaseGrant/allScopes/idsScope 的关系或替代路径。；证据：E12（app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authorization/references/fluent-registration.md:3–13）
- 已有能力与推荐用法：公开 defineDatabasePermission(...).build() 返回 PermissionGrant 可覆盖集合授权；但 AND 组合与 idsScope 无公开 helper，导出面与实现面仍不一致。；证据：E8（packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authorization/dist/server/index.d.ts:12–15）、E12（app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authorization/references/fluent-registration.md:3–13）

反馈复核是对原始反馈的证据核对，不等于人工确认、上游修复状态或自动修复授权；保留原评审置信度、分类与处理状态。

server/index.d.ts 导出 defineDatabasePermission、DatabasePermissionBuilderDefinition、recordAccess、anyScope、condition、scopeAst（E8），却未导出 databaseGrant、allScopes、idsScope；这些函数在实现中存在且语义清晰（E9/E10）。fluent-registration 指引推荐 defineDatabasePermission 与 defineRecordAccess（E12），应用据此接入，但仍手写 databaseGrant 形状与 allOf（E14）。

**影响**：应用需要复制库内小工具并自担语义（例如 allOf 与 anyScope 的差别），存在把 observerVisible OR not confidential 这类组合写错的风险；导出面与实现面不一致。

**建议改动**：从 @nocobase/app-plugin-authorization/server 导出 databaseGrant、allScopes、idsScope（或明确标注为内部并仅保留定义式 API），并在 fluent-registration 中说明二者关系。

**验收标准**：应用可用公开导出构造相同授权而无需自定义 helper；删除应用内 databaseGrant/allOf 后 B04/B05 仍通过。

作为 guidance-gap 型改进，材料足以证实改进空间。contract：sharing-rules SKILL 定义共享规则只向已持有该动作的主体追加所选记录、不能选择全部记录，并要求先经权限集授予动作；插件公开导出 sharingRules。behavior：应用自建 ticket_shares 并在数据作用域/记录访问解析中合并，B04 passed。application：应用未使用 sharingRules 而自建共享实体（材料未记录其是否评估过 defineSharingRule）。environment：B04 passed，非环境问题。factory：该 Skill 全文没有『何时应自建共享实体而非 defineSharingRule』的决策小节，也未给出与自定义共享实体的组合建议——这正是文档侧可改进点，且建议同时包含『何时自建』的分支，故即使出现能力不匹配，指引补齐仍然成立。existing-capability：公开的 sharingRules/defineSharingRule 可对已持动作主体共享指定记录；材料不足以证明它能否覆盖应用与自定义记录访问规则交织的临时只读场景，因此无法推翻『需要决策指引』的结论。原 finding 中『未说明主体本无该动作时该怎么做』的表述偏强，Skill 第 2 步已要求先授予动作，但不影响核心决策缺口成立。

  • 职责与公开约定：SKILL 声明共享只为已持有该动作的主体追加所选记录、不能选全部记录，并要求先经权限集授予动作；插件公开导出 sharingRules。 E13 E11
  • 实际行为证据：应用自建 ticket_shares 并在查询/记录访问解析中合并共享结果，B04 通过，未见使用 sharingRules。 E14 E15
  • 应用接入与业务边界：应用以自定义共享实体满足临时只读需求；材料未记录其是否评估或不能使用 defineSharingRule。 E14
  • 环境因素：B04 验收 passed、无控制台/页面错误，说明不是外部服务或运行环境导致的自建选择。 E15
  • 工厂因素：工厂提供的 sharing-rules SKILL 缺少内置共享规则与自建共享实体的适用边界/决策小节，也未给组合示例。 E13
  • 已有能力与推荐用法：defineSharingRule 与 sharingRules 公开存在，可对已持动作主体共享指定记录；但其能否覆盖应用这种与自定义记录访问解析交织的临时只读未被材料证实。 E11 E13

sharing-rules SKILL 说明共享规则只向已持有该动作的主体追加所选记录、不能选择全部记录（E13），插件导出 sharingRules（E11）。应用在 repair 轮读取过该 Skill，仍自建 ticket_shares 并在查询层合并（E14），B04 通过（E15）。仅从材料无法判断是能力不匹配还是未发现更合适的接入方式。

实际影响
Agent 需要自行判断共享语义与权限模型，容易出现重复自建或选择不合适的扩展点。
建议改法
在 sharing-rules Skill/参考中加入决策小节：何时用 defineSharingRule、何时需要独立共享实体，并给出记录选择与动作授予的边界示例。
诊断 · 触发条件、应有行为、绕行代价与验收标准
触发条件
需求要求“对没有该动作权限的工程师临时只读共享”，需要判断内置共享规则是否适用。
应有能力 / 行为
指引明确内置共享规则与自建共享表的适用边界及组合方式。

改进后的验收标准

共享规则用于向已持有该动作的主体追加所选记录，不能借此选择全部记录。

---
name: nocobase-app-plugin-authz-sharing-rules
description: Design, implement and verify sharing rules for NocoBase 3 business operations, including named data scopes and production administration.
---

# Develop sharing rules

A sharing rule adds selected records, or a record access selection, for the subjects it lists, provided they already hold the action. Sharing does not grant the operation, fields, page access or related records, and it cannot select all records. Grants, default access and sharing combine first; restriction rules then narrow the result.

Read the installed `nocobase-app-plugin-authorization` Skill first for composite resources, data scopes, fields, server policy enforcement and inherited subjects. The package README at `node_modules/@nocobase/app-plugin-authz-sharing-rules/README.md` is the complete reference for the service, the HTTP routes and the exports; this Skill covers how to use them.

For sharing between departments, read the application development Skill's `references/organization/permission-design.md` first. A rule whose recipient is a department shares specific records with it; a viewer-relative department scope such as 本部门及下属部门 is computed for the viewer and cannot select another department's records. The guide's optional sharing section covers this plugin, and its core section says what remains with permission sets alone.

## Development workflow

1. Identify the real collaboration exception: who receives which records, for which actions, and why. Use explicit records for one handover; use a record access selection for a maintained region or team rule.
2. Grant the recipients the business action through a permission set first. Register inherited subjects and their active membership resolver through the main authorization Skill.
3. Inspect every data scope the operation requires. Quote submission needs the selected quote and its actual parent project. Add each action and data scope deliberately; view, edit and submit are different permissions.
4. Save a complete rule through the settings page or the service. Keep record ids tied to their data scope's collection and preserve the other entries on update.
5. Verify the handover works, an unshared parent still blocks the workflow, restrictions still exclude confidential records, and sharing alone cannot activate a missing operation.
6. Revoke the recipient or the team's permission set and verify the next request loses only that source. Independent direct-user sharing and direct assignments must survive.

For example, a quote handover shares quote edit and submit plus the parent project's submit scope with a delegated engineer. Use the real record ids, never sample ones.

## Install

Register the default export of `@nocobase/app-plugin-authz-sharing-rules/client` in `client/plugins.ts` and of `@nocobase/app-plugin-authz-sharing-rules/server` in `server/plugins.ts`, beside the main authorization plugin, and run the application's migrations. Add the factory to the `plugins` of `server/config/authorization.ts`, merged with the application's other rule factories:

```ts
import { sharingRules } from '@nocobase/app-plugin-authz-sharing-rules/server';
app/server/service-authorization.ts:67–101E14

 * `databaseGrant` helper is not part of its public server entry point, so the
 * equally small public shape it builds is written here.

function databaseGrant(
  collection: string,
  definition: Readonly<Record<string, Record<string, unknown>>>,
): PermissionGrant {

    resource: { type: 'database.collection', id: collection },
    actions: Object.entries(definition).map(([action, config]) => ({
      action,
      policy: { type: 'database', ...config },
    })),
  };
}

function anyOf(field: string, values: readonly unknown[]): DatabaseScope {
  return anyScope(
    values.map((value) => condition(field, '$eq', value as never)),
  );
}

 * Every given condition has to hold. The public server entry point exports
 * `anyScope` (an OR of scopes) and `condition`, but not the library's own
 * `allScopes`, so the `and` group it would build is written here for the one
 * rule that needs two conditions to hold together. Note that an observer
 * scope built with `anyScope` would be `observerVisible OR not confidential`,
 * which lets a confidential ticket through as soon as it is observer-visible.
 */

        "以 Li Engineer A 打开保密工单 SR-20261009-0006，核对可正常处理",
        "以 Zhao Observer（svc.observer）打开工单列表与 SR-20261009-0005 详情，核对内部字段；以主管打开同一工单对比真实备注"
上游 Issue 草稿 · Markdown
### [信息][指引缺失] 共享规则 Skill 未说明何时应自建共享表而非使用 defineSharingRule

**相关对象**：`@nocobase/app-plugin-authz-sharing-rules`（评审时 1.0.0-beta.9）、`app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authz-sharing-rules/SKILL.md`

**反馈证据核对**：反馈复核：证据支持

作为 guidance-gap 型改进，材料足以证实改进空间。contract：sharing-rules SKILL 定义共享规则只向已持有该动作的主体追加所选记录、不能选择全部记录，并要求先经权限集授予动作；插件公开导出 sharingRules。behavior：应用自建 ticket_shares 并在数据作用域/记录访问解析中合并，B04 passed。application：应用未使用 sharingRules 而自建共享实体（材料未记录其是否评估过 defineSharingRule）。environment：B04 passed，非环境问题。factory：该 Skill 全文没有『何时应自建共享实体而非 defineSharingRule』的决策小节，也未给出与自定义共享实体的组合建议——这正是文档侧可改进点，且建议同时包含『何时自建』的分支，故即使出现能力不匹配，指引补齐仍然成立。existing-capability：公开的 sharingRules/defineSharingRule 可对已持动作主体共享指定记录；材料不足以证明它能否覆盖应用与自定义记录访问规则交织的临时只读场景，因此无法推翻『需要决策指引』的结论。原 finding 中『未说明主体本无该动作时该怎么做』的表述偏强，Skill 第 2 步已要求先授予动作，但不影响核心决策缺口成立。

- 职责与公开约定：SKILL 声明共享只为已持有该动作的主体追加所选记录、不能选全部记录，并要求先经权限集授予动作；插件公开导出 sharingRules。；证据：E13（app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authz-sharing-rules/SKILL.md:1–30）、E11（packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authz-sharing-rules/dist/server/index.d.ts:1–2）
- 实际行为证据：应用自建 ticket_shares 并在查询/记录访问解析中合并共享结果，B04 通过，未见使用 sharingRules。；证据：E14（app/server/service-authorization.ts:67–101）、E15（artifacts/verify-7/browser-acceptance/report.json:85–95）
- 应用接入与业务边界：应用以自定义共享实体满足临时只读需求；材料未记录其是否评估或不能使用 defineSharingRule。；证据：E14（app/server/service-authorization.ts:67–101）
- 环境因素：B04 验收 passed、无控制台/页面错误，说明不是外部服务或运行环境导致的自建选择。；证据：E15（artifacts/verify-7/browser-acceptance/report.json:85–95）
- 工厂因素：工厂提供的 sharing-rules SKILL 缺少内置共享规则与自建共享实体的适用边界/决策小节，也未给组合示例。；证据：E13（app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authz-sharing-rules/SKILL.md:1–30）
- 已有能力与推荐用法：defineSharingRule 与 sharingRules 公开存在，可对已持动作主体共享指定记录；但其能否覆盖应用这种与自定义记录访问解析交织的临时只读未被材料证实。；证据：E11（packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authz-sharing-rules/dist/server/index.d.ts:1–2）、E13（app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authz-sharing-rules/SKILL.md:1–30）

反馈复核是对原始反馈的证据核对，不等于人工确认、上游修复状态或自动修复授权；保留原评审置信度、分类与处理状态。

sharing-rules SKILL 说明共享规则只向已持有该动作的主体追加所选记录、不能选择全部记录（E13），插件导出 sharingRules（E11）。应用在 repair 轮读取过该 Skill，仍自建 ticket_shares 并在查询层合并（E14），B04 通过（E15）。仅从材料无法判断是能力不匹配还是未发现更合适的接入方式。

**影响**：Agent 需要自行判断共享语义与权限模型，容易出现重复自建或选择不合适的扩展点。

**建议改动**：在 sharing-rules Skill/参考中加入决策小节：何时用 defineSharingRule、何时需要独立共享实体，并给出记录选择与动作授予的边界示例。

**验收标准**：按更新后的指引能在不写额外共享表的前提下实现同类临时只读（或指引明确说明何时自建）；回归 B04 仍通过。

**评审时证据（冻结快照）**：
- `packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authz-sharing-rules/dist/server/index.d.ts:1–2` — sharing-rules 插件导出 default plugin 与 sharingRules 授权声明。
- `app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authz-sharing-rules/SKILL.md:1–30` — 共享规则用于向已持有该动作的主体追加所选记录，不能借此选择全部记录。
- `app/server/service-authorization.ts:67–101` — 应用注释说明 databaseGrant 非公开入口故自行重写形状，allScopes 非公开故手写 allOf，并提示 anyScope 组合观察员范围可能放行保密工单。
- `artifacts/verify-7/browser-acceptance/report.json:85–95` — B04 status=passed，覆盖甲乙工程师、临时共享/撤销、保密工单与观察员备注隔离。

本轮过程记录

本轮修复（第 5 轮）：两个 QA 反馈缺陷。缺陷一（受理工作流分支）：supervisor 受理工单后，工作流未按普通/紧急分支产生不同受理说明，普通与紧急工单都收到相同的 ticket.assigned 通知，workflow 图中也没有优先级分支节点。修复：在 workflows/ticket-acceptance 新增 decide-acceptance-priority 条件节点，yes 走 apply-urgent-acceptance（受理说明含“紧急”）、no 走 apply-normal-acceptance（含“普通”），两者调用同一 applyAcceptance 状态迁移，仅 note 与通知不同；tokens.ts 的 applyAcceptance 端口新增 note?；删除旧的单一 apply-acceptance.ts；server/service/ticket-service.ts 的 applyAcceptance 接受 note，写入受理日志并把 owner 通知标题按优先级加【紧急】前缀，body 用对应受理说明（kind 仍为 ticket.assigned）。缺陷二（客户/设备与 Owner 显示、共享工单设备访问）：工单列表/详情不显示所属客户与设备，Owner 显示为 UUID；临时共享未把该工单的客户/设备基础信息带给共享工程师，共享工程师的设备列表也缺该设备。修复：server/service/ticket-service.ts 新增 describeTickets，按调用者已可读记录解析 customerName/deviceSerial/deviceName/ownerName（observer 被隐藏的 ownerId 不解析，保持“—”），listShares 解析 engineerName；routes 的列表/详情返回 enrich 后的记录；server/service-authorization.ts 的 devicesOf 并入 active ticket_shares 指向的工单设备（innerJoin tickets），共享只开放该工单所需的一台设备，撤销（active=false）即收回且不改动其它工单/整套客户档案；client 列表新增客户/设备列，详情新增客户/设备行、Owner 显示 ownerName，共享列表显示工程师名；client/locales/{en-US,zh-CN}.ts 补 service.tickets.customer/device。验证：tests/logic/service-equipment.test.ts 新增 3 个用例（优先级分支说明/通知不同；共享获得客户/设备且撤销收回、只读被拒；保密工单拒绝共享），全套 31 用例通过；新增 tests/components/service-tickets.test.tsx（真实页面渲染，列表与详情显示客户/设备/Owner 名）2 用例通过；app-locale-coverage、service-attachment-validation、server-imports 通过；tsconfig.server.json 与 client tsc --noEmit 通过；改动文件 prettier/eslint 通过。未修改工厂控制文件、未回滚应用、未弱化验证。 补充修复：EquipmentWorkflowProvider 原先只要存在 enabled+current 版本就提前返回，导致本轮工作流源码变更在既有数据库上不会激活新 Artifact；改为对比 current.hash 与本次 digest，不一致时清空该 key 并启用本次 materialize 的 revision，新增 tests/logic/equipment-workflow-provider.test.ts 4 个用例。

问题 01 · 修复阶段

Registry 扩展的 AI 组件文案未进入应用 locale，app-locale-coverage 报 139 个缺失键

处理结果未提供

tests/logic/app-locale-coverage.test.ts 的 'translates every key the application uses into English/Chinese' 失败：expected [ …(139) ] to deeply equal []，缺失项全部来自 extensions/nocobase-ai/components/**（chat / tools / surfaces / triggers / page-elements）。

原因（实现者自述）
模板的 app-locale-coverage 测试扫描整个 client/**/*.tsx?，只跳过 /locales/ 目录和自身含 useTranslation('…') 的文件。Registry 项 nocobase-ai 的组件通过自带的 useAITranslate(key, fallback) 翻译，文案存在扩展自己的 locales/en-US.ts、zh-CN.ts 中，既不在 client/locales，组件文件也不含 useTranslation('…')，因此所有键都被判定为应用文案缺失。此外该扩展自带 locale 有 4 个键（tool.output、tool.businessReport.chartFailed、tool.businessReport.previewUnavailable、tool.businessReport.failed）只在代码里以内联英文 fallback 存在，中文界面下会显示英文。
处理方式
在 client/locales/en-US.ts 与 zh-CN.ts 中直接展开（import + spread）扩展自带的 en-US/zh-CN 资源，使应用命名空间覆盖这些键，不手抄、不重复维护；同时把 4 个缺失键补进扩展的 en-US.ts 与 zh-CN.ts，中英键集保持一致（各 166 键）。未改动测试、未删除任何键——校验强度不变。
影响 / 代价：约 15 分钟定位 + 修复来源：Agent · retro.json
问题 02 · 修复阶段

业务实现整体缺失：仅模板首页，客户/设备/工单/巡检/资料/助手/看板/外部接口均无页面与数据

本任务已处理

QA 反馈：登录后顶栏仅 Home；直接打开 /main/serviceWorkOrders、/main/work-orders、/main/customers 均回落到默认模板首页；设置-Automation-Workflows 显示 No data to display；设置-Automation-Scheduled tasks 显示 No scheduled tasks are defined；设置-AI-Knowledge bases 显示 No knowledge bases yet；设置-AI-Employees 只有内置 Atlas；设置-User management 只有 Super Admin，设置-Authorization-Permission Sets 只有内置 Member 与 System administrator；工单详情/附件/站内消息/统计看板均不存在；/api/serviceWorkOrders 等业务接口 404；数据库浏览器里 serviceCustomers/serviceDevices/serviceWorkOrders（字段以 service_* 形式）存在但没有任何页面。

原因（实现者自述）
基线之上只有未提交、未接线的半成品：两个 202610080001/2 迁移（创建 service_* 集合）与 server/service/resources.ts，它们不被任何 provider/route 引用；client/extensions/nocobase-ai 只是被复制进来；缺少全部用户可见部分（client 页面与 client/routes.ts 导航、database/main/seeds 种子、server/routes/*、server/service/* 领域服务、workflows/ 源、Scheduler 目标、权限集与记录访问规则），所以表存在但应用没有入口。
处理方式
移植同一基线（30161f6）上已完成且自测通过的 issue-666 实现（commit 94285e4，tree 中 63 个新增/修改文件）到工作树：client/routes.ts 的 service 导航组与 10 个业务页面（dashboard、customers、devices、tickets 列表+详情、inspections、knowledge、manuals、assistant、messages、operations）；6 个迁移 + 1 个幂等种子（3 客户、6 设备、6 工单含普通/紧急/保密/超期、1 巡检、2 维修知识、2 手册、5 个演示账号 + 4 个权限集）；server/routes/* 与 server/service/* 领域服务；workflows/ticket-acceptance 受理工作流；Scheduler 两个计划目标；权限集与记录访问规则；附件内容校验与外部报修接口。同时删除原先未接线的 674 残留（service_* 迁移、server/service/resources.ts、nocobase-ai 扩展）。验证：pnpm typecheck、pnpm build 通过；vitest 全量 43 文件 276 用例通过（含 tests/logic/service-equipment.test.ts 27 用例、app-locale-coverage、client-routes）；AUTH_SECRET 方式 pnpm dev 启动，种子账号 svc.supervisor 登录成功，/api/service/{me,tickets,customers,devices} 返回种子数据，SQLite 中 workflows=1、schedule_definitions=2、manuals=2、knowledge_articles=2、user=6、tickets=6、customers=3、devices=6、inspections=1；已停止自启服务。

问题 03 · 修复阶段

设置-AI-Knowledge bases 仍为空、设置-AI-Employees 仍只有内置 Atlas，未注册服务助手

未解决

QA 前置动作会打开 设置-AI-Knowledge bases（仍显示 No knowledge bases yet）与 设置-AI-Employees（只有 Atlas）。原任务要求用现有 AI Knowledge Base 维护内部设备手册知识库、用现有 AI Employee 提供服务助手。

处理方式
本轮未处理。若下一轮要求 AI 设置页非空且员工可见，需在具备 embedding 服务与 pgvector 的环境按 nocobase-app-plugin-ai-knowledge-base / ai-employee Skill 配置 config.yml、写 Manifest 并注册 AI Employee；缺少这些条件时真实检索/问答仍应记 blocked/not_run，不能用固定答案冒充。

问题 04 · 修复阶段

专用集成用户 svc.integration 无法取得 API 密钥：设置页只给当前管理员自助建密钥，运维页只列不建，账号又进不了插件页

本任务已处理

QA：专用集成用户 svc.integration 登录后设置页提示无可用设置（no accessible settings），用户菜单没有密钥入口；设置→API 密钥 只能给当前登录管理员创建密钥且只有名称/有效期，无法选择密钥归属；运维页（service-operations）列出了 service-integration 账号的密钥并提示「在 设置→API 密钥 中创建」，但那里无法为集成账号创建，也没有任何其他入口，与运维页文案矛盾。

原因（实现者自述）
Better Auth 的 /api/auth/api-key/* 与 app-plugin-api-keys 的设置页都是自助语义：只按调用者会话操作自己的密钥，supervisor 对他人密钥调用会 404，页面也没有身份选择。应用此前只补了 supervisor 侧的「列出/撤销」可信操作（server/routes/service-api-keys.ts）和运维页对应 UI，缺失「为指定集成账号签发」的可信入口；同时 service-integration / service-supervisor 权限集都没有 page:api-keys/access，所以插件页面根本不出现在这些账号的可用设置里。
处理方式
复用现有 API Keys 插件能力：在 server/routes/service-api-keys.ts 增加 GET /service/integration/accounts（supervisor 列出持有 service-integration 权限集的账号）与 POST /service/integration/api-keys（supervisor 为选定集成账号调用 ApiKeyService.create，返回一次性 secret，拒绝非集成账号 404），与既有列出/撤销共用 requireSupervisor 与 ApiKeyService；在 server/service-authorization.ts 给 supervisor 与 integration 权限集加 pageGrant('api-keys')，使插件页对它们可见（integration 只能管理自己的密钥）；client/pages/service/operations.tsx 增加签发表单（账号选择、名称、可选 1–365 天有效期）与一次性密钥展示；修正 client/locales/{en-US,zh-CN}.ts 中误导性的提示文案。
影响 / 代价：未提供，不估算来源：Agent · retro.json
问题 05 · 修复阶段

B06 有效 DOCX 维修报告被客户端判为无效：上传选择被同步清空、空字节读取被直接拒绝

本任务已处理

QA 在工单详情把有效 sample.docx 以 Report 分类上传，界面提示『Only a valid PNG photo or DOCX report can be attached. The file was not saved.』，同页 PNG 上传成功；应用日志没有 DOCX 的 POST /api/ticketAttachments/uploadOne，说明被客户端校验拦截。

原因（实现者自述）
validateAttachmentFile 读取 file.slice(0,8) 后做 PNG/ZIP 头部判断；但 detail.tsx 的 input onChange 在调用异步 upload 前先执行 event.target.value=''，upload 的 finally 又清空一次。同步清空 FileList 会与异步字节读取竞争，使有效 DOCX 读到空头部，被 ATTACHMENT_CONTENT_INVALID 拒绝；空读取也没有与『内容确实不符』区分开。校验失败后选择即被清空，input.files.length 恒为 0，误导自动化观察。
处理方式
client/pages/service/tickets/detail.tsx：删除 change 内与 finally 内的同步清空，改为 onClick（选择器打开时）清空，使同一文件可重选，且校验失败保留选择、成功后 input.files 仍反映真实选择；移除已无用的 fileInputRef。client/pages/service/service-api.ts：png/docx 头部读取为空时 return，交由服务端 assertAttachmentContent 依据真实字节复检并清理残留、返回同一错误文案。tests/logic/service-attachment-validation.test.ts 增加空读取 defer 用例。未弱化服务端校验、未改回滚、未动工厂控制文件。
影响 / 代价：未提供，不估算来源：Agent · retro.json
问题 06 · 修复阶段

受理工作流未按普通/紧急分支：普通与紧急工单得到相同受理说明与相同的“新工单待处理”通知

本任务已处理

QA 运行 #2 输入 priority=urgent，受理成功，但 work order 得到的 ticket.assigned 通知与普通工单完全一致（“新工单待处理 …”），workflow 运行图中只有 loadTicket → decideAcceptance → applyAcceptance，没有优先级分支节点，普通/紧急受理说明无差异。

原因（实现者自述）
workflows/ticket-acceptance/workflow.ts 在 decideAcceptance 的 yes 分支上只挂了一个 applyAcceptance 运行节点，受理说明与通知文案写死在 service.applyAcceptance 内，与优先级无关；DSL 中不存在可按 priority 路由的条件节点，因此无论普通还是紧急都走同一条路径。
处理方式
在 workflow.ts 的 yes 分支内新增条件节点 decideAcceptancePriority（标题 Decide acceptance note by priority），yes=[applyUrgentAcceptance]、no=[applyNormalAcceptance]；新增 server/decide-acceptance-priority.ts（input.priority === "urgent"）、server/apply-normal-acceptance.ts（NORMAL_ACCEPTANCE_NOTE 含“普通”）、server/apply-urgent-acceptance.ts（URGENT_ACCEPTANCE_NOTE 含“紧急”）；两个运行节点都调用同一 service.applyAcceptance，仅 note 不同，保证状态迁移一致而说明可区分。tokens.ts 的 applyAcceptance 端口新增 note?: string；删除不再被引用的旧 apply-acceptance.ts。server/service/ticket-service.ts 的 applyAcceptance 接受 note，写入受理日志（step=accepted,status=success,message=“<note> (by=… owner=…)”），owner 通知标题在 priority==="urgent" 时加【紧急】前缀，body 用对应受理说明，kind 仍为 ticket.assigned。
影响 / 代价：未提供，不估算来源：Agent · retro.json
问题 07 · 修复阶段

工单列表/详情不显示客户与设备、Owner 显示 UUID；临时共享未携带客户/设备基础信息，共享工程师设备列表缺该设备

本任务已处理

QA：工单详情与列表看不到所属客户与设备；Owner 显示为 UUID 而非姓名；工程师 B 的设备列表为 3/4/8，但临时共享给 B 的工单 SR-20261009-0002 的设备（id=2 JMP-CNC-002）不在其中；共享没有把该工单的客户/设备基础信息带给 B；保密工单不应出现临时共享区块。

原因（实现者自述）
工单接口只返回原始 customerId/deviceId/ownerId，前端没有把 id 解析成名称的通道，也没有任何地方解析它们；Owner 直接渲染 ownerId。共享只写入 ticket_shares 行并让授权允许读取工单本体，但 devicesOf 的记录范围仅来自 owner 的 tickets 与 inspections，未计入 active 的 ticket_shares，所以共享不带来设备（以及经 devicesRelated 的客户基础信息）。
处理方式
server/service/ticket-service.ts 新增 TicketRelations 与 describeTickets(records)：仅在调用者已经可读的记录上用 raw query 解析 customers.name、devices.serial/name、user.name（ownerId 被字段级授权隐藏时不解析，observer 保持“—”而不是泄漏）；listShares 解析 engineerName。server/routes/service-tickets.ts 的列表与详情返回 describeTickets 后的记录。server/service-authorization.ts 的 devicesOf 并入 active ticket_shares 指向的 tickets.device_id（innerJoin ticket_shares），共享只开放该工单所需的一台设备及其客户基础信息，不开放该客户其它设备或其它工单；撤销（active=false）后不再计入。client/pages/service 的列表新增 Customer/Device 列、详情新增 Customer/Device 行、Owner 显示 ownerName、共享列表显示 engineerName；client/locales/{en-US,zh-CN}.ts 补 service.tickets.customer/device。
影响 / 代价：未提供，不估算来源：Agent · retro.json
问题 08 · 修复阶段

受理工作流修复在既有数据库上不生效：EquipmentWorkflowProvider 只要发现已启用的 current 版本就直接跳过，不会切到本次构建的新 Artifact

本任务已处理

本轮缺陷一修复后工作流源码产生新的 Artifact digest，但在已经运行过上一构建、workflows 表中存在 enabled=true、current=true 旧版本的数据库上重启应用时，provider 命中 `if (current) return;` 提前返回，新版本被 materialize 为 enabled=false，旧版本继续作为 current 执行，受理分支修复不会生效。

原因（实现者自述）
server/providers/equipment-workflow-provider.ts 只按 enabled+current 查询是否存在，未对比该版本的 hash 是否就是本次 discoverArtifacts 得到的 digest；而 loader.ensureMaterialized 明确不激活新版本（materializeWorkflowSource 创建 enabled:false,current:null），因此旧版本永远不会被替换。
处理方式
provider 改为读取 current.hash，仅当它等于本次构建的 artifact.digest 时才保持不动；否则按原有逻辑先 updateMany 清空该 key 的 current/enabled，再 updateOne 把本次 materialize 得到的 workflowId 置为 current+enabled。新增 tests/logic/equipment-workflow-provider.test.ts（单元用例：旧 digest 被替换、本构建 digest 保持不变、插件缺失/Artifact 缺失时不做任何操作），4 用例通过。未修改工厂控制文件。
影响 / 代价：未提供，不估算来源：Agent · retro.json

运行记录 · 非根因分析

实现者补充建议

来自可选过程笔记，不代表已独立确认；相同建议引用上方评审，其余保留原文。

建议 01 · 验收机制

app-locale-coverage 把 UI Library Registry 扩展的文案误判为应用文案

可自动化

测试按目录/命名空间跳过文件：只认 useTranslation('…')，且只在 /locales/ 目录下跳过。Registry 项（如 nocobase-ai）用模块级 hook useAITranslate 并自带 locales/，其 components/** 仍被扫描，任何安装该扩展的应用都会凭空多出上百个「缺失键」，只能把整份扩展文案复制进应用命名空间或改扩展源码，二者都会造成漂移。

建议改动

在 tests/logic/app-locale-coverage.test.ts 的 sourceFiles() 或 referencedKeys() 中一并跳过 client/extensions/**（它们是 UI Library/Registry 源码，其文案由扩展自己的 locales/ 拥有），或识别 useAITranslate 这类模块级翻译 hook；并在 i18n 参考文档中说明扩展自有文案不计入应用 locale 覆盖。

来源：Agent · retro.json

建议 02 · 模板兼容

@nocobase/app-plugin-ai-employee 的 Registry 项自身 locale 缺少内联 fallback 键

可自动化

扩展多个组件调用 t(key, '英文 fallback')，但 registry/nocobase-ai/locales/{en-US,zh-CN}.ts 里没有这些键，切到中文时这些提示仍是英文。应用侧只能事后补齐，属于上游包缺陷。

建议改动

在 app-plugin-ai-employee 的源码/Registry 生成流程中加一个测试：扫描 registry/nocobase-ai/components/** 里 useAITranslate 的 key，断言每个 key 在扩展自身 en-US.ts 与 zh-CN.ts 中都有条目；并把 tool.output、tool.businessReport.chartFailed、tool.businessReport.previewUnavailable、tool.businessReport.failed 补进随包 locale。

来源：Agent · retro.json

建议 03 · 模板兼容

@nocobase/app-plugin-api-keys 只有自助入口，缺少按身份管理的公开契约

可自动化

设置→API 密钥 页面与 /api/auth/api-key/* 都只作用于调用者自身会话；当应用需要为专用集成账号签发/撤销密钥时，只能自己 join apikey 与 user 表列出所有者，并直接构造 ApiKeyService 这个较底层的服务端对象。Authorization 已内建 page:api-keys/access 页面权限，但页面本身没有身份选择。

建议改动

让 api-keys 插件公开受授权保护的按身份管理契约（如 ApiKeyService.listForUser/createForUser/removeForUser），并在页面为有权限的管理员提供账号选择；或至少在 Skill 中明确说明应用需自行实现可信管理入口的方式与可用的公开服务。

来源：Agent · retro.json

建议 04 · 验收机制

agent-browser upload 对不存在的路径静默设置 0 字节文件，易被误判为应用拒绝有效文件

可自动化

agent-browser 0.36.0 的 upload（CDP DOM.setFileInputFiles）在路径不存在/无法解析时既不报错也不回退，而是给 input 设置一个 name/MIME 正确但 size=0 的 File。随后应用按内容校验会以『无效文件』拒绝，且不产生任何网络请求，外观上与『有效文件被错误拒绝』完全一致。本轮在 verify-4 复现：upload #f /tmp/does-not-exist/sample.docx 与相对路径 upload #f sample.docx 均得到 {size:0}。

建议改动

上传前断言路径存在，或在 upload 后读取 input.files[0].size 并在为 0 时以明确错误退出；为 upload 记录解析后的绝对路径。fixtures 始终使用绝对路径。

来源：Agent · retro.json

原始搭建历史覆盖

最突出的问题是 Workflow 部署激活与 Scheduler 按需执行缺少公开入口：应用只能 cast 内部 token、反射私有 runner 并直写物化表，与 Skill 自身“勿绕过内部/物化表”的要求冲突（F3/F4），使插件内部布局成为事实接口、重构风险高；次级问题是 authorization 导出面与实现面不一致（F5）及共享规则指引未覆盖自建共享表的选择（F6）。可用的能力是站内通知 send 与 Scheduler 注册/声明入口，均被按文档正确采用（F1/F2）。覆盖限制：仅评四个能力单元，File/API keys/AI employee 未评估，业务 QA 通过不等于框架充分。

Workflow 审批分支与部署激活对象、需求覆盖、五项理由与证据

工单受理的 DSL 条件分支执行、trigger 幂等、运行记录，以及随源码部署的 workflow 版本的启动激活。

插件@nocobase/app-plugin-workflow2.0.0-beta.0

@nocobase/app-plugin-workflow/server：workflowServiceToken 与 WorkflowServiceContract · WorkflowServiceContract.registerInstruction()/trigger() · WorkflowService.discoverArtifacts()/ensureArtifactMaterialized()

E1 E2 E3
指引 / Skillapp/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-workflow/SKILL.md版本见冻结输入

使用公开包导出与 API，不绕过插件内部或物化表 · 业务调用只走 workflowServiceToken.trigger；管理/启停留给带权限的管理 API

E4
指引 / Skillapp/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-workflow/references/invocation-and-service-api.md版本见冻结输入

public server entry 只导出 workflowServiceToken · 服务方法映射与 enable/管理 API 边界

E5

需求 → 框架职责 → 推荐与实际接入

普通与紧急工单各受理一次，产生对应 Workflow 执行记录与分支说明，重复受理不重复执行或通知。直接支持
框架职责与业务边界
框架负责 workflowServiceToken.trigger 的公开调用、DSL 条件分支执行、运行记录与事件去重；优先级判定与受理幂等属应用业务规则。
推荐使用方式
业务代码 resolve workflowServiceToken 并调用 trigger(workflowKey, input, options?)；复用稳定事件键实现去重，分支用 DSL condition 表达。
Agent 实际使用
应用以 workflow DSL 声明 ticket-acceptance，受理逻辑经服务触发；verify-6 首轮因应用自身优先级分支缺陷 B02 失败，repair-5 修正后 verify-7 B02 两分支通过。
缺口归因
未记录能力缺口
E1 E2 E5 E7
应用启动时自动启用已随源码部署的 workflow 版本，使安装即可运行。能力缺口
框架职责与业务边界
框架应提供公开的服务级入口来启用/激活 source-managed workflow 版本；workflow 的业务内容属应用。
推荐使用方式
SKILL 要求使用公开导出、禁止绕过插件内部或直接改物化表；但公开契约只有 registerInstruction/trigger，enable 只在带权限的管理 API 与包内服务方法。
Agent 实际使用
EquipmentWorkflowProvider 从 @nocobase/app-plugin-workflow/server 导入 workflowServiceToken，自定义 WorkflowActivationRuntime 结构 cast token，调用 discoverArtifacts/ensureArtifactMaterialized 并直接改 workflows 表标记 current/enabled。
缺口归因
NocoBase3 插件
E3 E4 E5 E6

74/100

E3 E4 E5

设计合理性

70/100

实现完整性与可靠性

这是业务验证背景，不直接换算框架分数；首轮失败不会被最终通过覆盖。

数据授权与记录共享对象、需求覆盖、五项理由与证据

按角色限制记录可见范围（工程师自有工单、观察员隐藏内部备注）、数据库级权限声明，以及工单的临时记录级共享与撤销。

E13

需求 → 框架职责 → 推荐与实际接入

工程师只能读自己工单、观察员看不到内部备注、主管可见范围正确，且知识库阅读资格可控。正常组合
框架职责与业务边界
框架提供数据库级权限声明、条件作用域与记录访问解析；角色-资源-动作的业务映射属应用。
推荐使用方式
fluent-registration 指引推荐 defineDatabasePermission(configure) 定义集合权限、defineRecordAccess 定义记录访问，并用 anyScope/condition 构造作用域。
Agent 实际使用
应用用 defineDatabasePermission 并自写 databaseGrant 形状与 allOf（因 databaseGrant/allScopes/idsScope 未从 server 入口导出）；B04/B05 最终通过。
缺口归因
NocoBase3 插件
E8 E9 E10 E12 E14 E15
普通工单临时共享给指定工程师只读、撤销后失效；保密工单不可因共享越权。正常组合
框架职责与业务边界
框架提供记录级共享/访问扩展点；何时使用内置共享规则与何时自建共享表属应用判断与指引覆盖。
推荐使用方式

证据与原文

E1 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-workflow/dist/server/tokens.d.ts:4–9

WorkflowServiceContract 只暴露 registerInstruction 与 trigger；internalWorkflowServiceToken 单独声明且未随公开契约导出。

export interface WorkflowServiceContract {
    registerInstruction(instruction: WorkflowInstructionClass): void;

/**
 * The workflow runtime methods this provider needs. `workflowServiceToken` is
 * published under the narrower `WorkflowServiceContract`; the service object
 * behind it also exposes artifact materialization, which is what activation
 * uses to create the revision row before marking it current and enabled.
 */
interface WorkflowActivationRuntime {
  discoverArtifacts(): Promise<readonly { key: string; digest: string }[]>;
  ensureArtifactMaterialized(digest: string): Promise<number | undefined>;
}

/**
 * Activates the application-owned ticket acceptance workflow.
 *
 * Workflow enablement is deliberately a management decision in the platform:
 * a discovered Artifact is materialized but stays disabled until someone
 * enables it. A business application that ships a source-managed workflow
 * still has to be operable the moment it is installed, so this provider
 * materializes the current Artifact and marks that revision current and
 * enabled. It is idempotent: an already enabled revision is left untouched,
 * and a rebuild that produces a new Artifact hash activates the new revision
 * instead of silently running the old one.
 *
 * This runs on every start, but does nothing when the workflow plugin is
 * absent, so the application still starts without it.
 */

E10 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authorization/dist/server/database/scope.js:32–47

allScopes/idsScope 在实现中存在（and 组合、按 id 展开 $eq），但未从公开入口导出。

export function allScopes(scopes) {
    if (scopes.includes(false))
        return false;
    const items = scopes.filter((scope) => scope !== true);
    if (items.length === 0)
        return true;
    return items.length === 1 ? items[0] : group('and', items);
}
/**
 * Membership expands to one `$eq` per identifier because `FilterOperator` has
 * no `$in` — `$includes` is a substring test. A negated list would expand the
 * same way, as an `and` of `$ne`. Both collapse once db grows the operator.
 */
export function idsScope(field, ids) {
    return anyScope(ids.map((id) => condition(field, '$eq', id)));
}

E12 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authorization/references/fluent-registration.md:3–13

推荐用定义式 defineDatabasePermission/defineRecordAccess 与 authz.database.collections.add 描述集合权限、数据作用域与记录访问。

Opt collections in with `authz.database.collections.add(...)`; pages need no server registration because the client route tree lists them. Define composite resources with `defineCompositeResource(name, configure)` from `@nocobase/authorization/core` and reusable collection permissions with `defineDatabasePermission(configure)` from `@nocobase/app-plugin-authorization/server`. Both callbacks execute synchronously and return immutable builders; neither registers anything, queries a database or assigns permissions. Register a built resource with `authz.compositeResources.define(resource)`, which returns its `CompositeResourceReference`, and list it in the workspace with `authz.ui.place(reference, { section })`; `resource.reference()` returns the same reference without registering.

Use the complete [quote resource declaration](runtime-api.md#declare-a-business-operation) as the starting point and the [permission-set declaration](code-and-seeds.md#share-declarations-not-runtime-instances) to select its data scopes. This reference adds builder semantics and relation capabilities. A composite can also be written as a plain object, `{ name, title, actions: [{ name, title, dataScopes, grants }] }`, where each data scope is `{ key, title, options?, defaultValue? }` and targets the one resource its grants name; the package README shows both forms.

The row type passed to `.collection<Row>(name)` supplies compile-time field checks, not another runtime schema. DB metadata remains authoritative. Collection registration defines which operations participate in authorization; the permission builder describes requested capabilities independently. The registered Collection action catalogue is enforced when authorizing requests.

`grant(key, permission, { title? })` binds a reusable permission to one data scope of the action. The key is stored as `scopeKey` and names the data scope a grant or rule fills with a record selection. The title defaults to the permission's explicit title, or the collection name; override it when one action uses the same collection twice. Different keys allow independent multi-collection configuration. Repeated keys and operations are rejected. A business action composes only `database.collection` grants.

`read` describes output fields, while `create` and `update` describe input fields. `delete()` takes no fields. Use `'*'` or `.allFields()` explicitly for all fields; for `create` and `update` that means every field a write may set, leaving out auto-increment, generated and optimistic-lock version columns. Never list one of those, or a field that does not exist, in a `create` or `update` list: db refuses the whole Policy, so every write would fail, and the startup scan reports it. `.options(...recordAccessReferences)` limits the record access a grant may choose and keeps the option names typed on the resource reference; `.default(reference)` sets the value used when a grant chooses nothing. The references must apply to the selected collection. Omitting options offers every registered record access that applies.

Define record access with `defineRecordAccess(key, configure)` from `@nocobase/authorization/core`, then register it with `authz.recordAccess.define(access)`. Use `.collections(...names)` for applicability (`'*'` for every collection), `.params<P>(schema)` for typed parameters and `.resolver(fn)` for evaluation. The resolver receives `{ principal, collection, action, params }` and no database objects; it returns `true`, `false`, a filter built with `condition(...)` from `@nocobase/app-plugin-authorization/server`, or a `FilterAst` built with `buildFilter` from `@nocobase/repository-input`, which the database adapter validates and compiles. Grants and rules never store resolver functions: `.options(...)`, `.default(...)`, relation `.recordAccess(...)` and `selection.recordAccess(key, params?)` store keys. Use stable keys because grants and rules persist them.

原文件 SHA-256：f1676311720ddb02310f8cd9b7b487b4a86a1f53a7a05b46afc3c26722deff6e

E13 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authz-sharing-rules/SKILL.md:1–30

共享规则用于向已持有该动作的主体追加所选记录，不能借此选择全部记录。

---
name: nocobase-app-plugin-authz-sharing-rules
description: Design, implement and verify sharing rules for NocoBase 3 business operations, including named data scopes and production administration.
---

# Develop sharing rules

A sharing rule adds selected records, or a record access selection, for the subjects it lists, provided they already hold the action. Sharing does not grant the operation, fields, page access or related records, and it cannot select all records. Grants, default access and sharing combine first; restriction rules then narrow the result.

Read the installed `nocobase-app-plugin-authorization` Skill first for composite resources, data scopes, fields, server policy enforcement and inherited subjects. The package README at `node_modules/@nocobase/app-plugin-authz-sharing-rules/README.md` is the complete reference for the service, the HTTP routes and the exports; this Skill covers how to use them.

For sharing between departments, read the application development Skill's `references/organization/permission-design.md` first. A rule whose recipient is a department shares specific records with it; a viewer-relative department scope such as 本部门及下属部门 is computed for the viewer and cannot select another department's records. The guide's optional sharing section covers this plugin, and its core section says what remains with permission sets alone.

## Development workflow

1. Identify the real collaboration exception: who receives which records, for which actions, and why. Use explicit records for one handover; use a record access selection for a maintained region or team rule.
2. Grant the recipients the business action through a permission set first. Register inherited subjects and their active membership resolver through the main authorization Skill.
3. Inspect every data scope the operation requires. Quote submission needs the selected quote and its actual parent project. Add each action and data scope deliberately; view, edit and submit are different permissions.
4. Save a complete rule through the settings page or the service. Keep record ids tied to their data scope's collection and preserve the other entries on update.
5. Verify the handover works, an unshared parent still blocks the workflow, restrictions still exclude confidential records, and sharing alone cannot activate a missing operation.
6. Revoke the recipient or the team's permission set and verify the next request loses only that source. Independent direct-user sharing and direct assignments must survive.

For example, a quote handover shares quote edit and submit plus the parent project's submit scope with a delegated engineer. Use the real record ids, never sample ones.

## Install

Register the default export of `@nocobase/app-plugin-authz-sharing-rules/client` in `client/plugins.ts` and of `@nocobase/app-plugin-authz-sharing-rules/server` in `server/plugins.ts`, beside the main authorization plugin, and run the application's migrations. Add the factory to the `plugins` of `server/config/authorization.ts`, merged with the application's other rule factories:

```ts
import { sharingRules } from '@nocobase/app-plugin-authz-sharing-rules/server';

原文件 SHA-256：7ef72078b29b536ef4ab5a4a129c12139d7be1a877d45dda5820864570d1f668

E14 · app/server/service-authorization.ts:67–101

应用注释说明 databaseGrant 非公开入口故自行重写形状，allScopes 非公开故手写 allOf，并提示 anyScope 组合观察员范围可能放行保密工单。

/**
 * A collection grant for the database permission type. The library's own
 * `databaseGrant` helper is not part of its public server entry point, so the
 * equally small public shape it builds is written here.
 */
function databaseGrant(
  collection: string,
  definition: Readonly<Record<string, Record<string, unknown>>>,
): PermissionGrant {
  return {
    resource: { type: 'database.collection', id: collection },
    actions: Object.entries(definition).map(([action, config]) => ({
      action,
      policy: { type: 'database', ...config },
    })),
  };
}

function anyOf(field: string, values: readonly unknown[]): DatabaseScope {
  return anyScope(
    values.map((value) => condition(field, '$eq', value as never)),
  );
}

/**
 * Every given condition has to hold. The public server entry point exports
 * `anyScope` (an OR of scopes) and `condition`, but not the library's own
 * `allScopes`, so the `and` group it would build is written here for the one
 * rule that needs two conditions to hold together. Note that an observer
 * scope built with `anyScope` would be `observerVisible OR not confidential`,
 * which lets a confidential ticket through as soon as it is observer-visible.
 */
function allOf(...scopes: readonly DatabaseScope[]): DatabaseScope {
  return { kind: 'group', logic: 'and', items: [...scopes] } as DatabaseScope;
}

export interface SchedulerService {
    /**
     * Registers what a schedule can point at: how its configuration is
     * validated, how a firing starts, and — for anything that finishes later —
     * how a run is inspected. The returned handle is how that target reports a
     * terminal outcome back.
     */
    registerTarget<TConfig extends JsonObject>(target: ScheduleTargetType<TConfig>): ScheduleTargetHandle;
    /**
     * Registers a scheduled task this app should run. Call during `register()`
     * or `boot()`, the same way as `registerTarget` — schedules are read once
     * when `sync()` runs during `start()`, so anything defined afterward has no
     * effect until the next sync. `key` is the schedule's stable, application-
     * wide identity.
     */
    defineSchedule(definition: ScheduleDefinition): void;
}
/**
 * The scheduler's one public service, carrying its one extension point.
 * Reading and changing schedules is reachable through the plugin's HTTP API
 * and its `scheduler sync` command; the store, the target registry and the
 * occurrence history stay private to this package.

    compose() {
        if (this.internals)
            return this.internals;
        const container = this.app.container;
        const database = container.resolve(databaseManagerToken);
        const targets = new ScheduleTargetRegistry();
        const occurrences = new ScheduleOccurrenceStore(database);
        // Concurrency and attempts are the selected configuration's. A failed
        // dispatch is recorded as the occurrence's outcome, so a retry of the same
        // firing finds it recorded and does nothing: keep attempts at 1 there.
        const executor = container
            .resolve(jobExecutorServiceToken)
            .getScheduleExecutor(SCHEDULER_SCOPE, this.jobsConfiguration());
        const store = new ScheduleStore(database, this.app.appName, executor, (spec, limit) => createScheduleDispatchJob(spec, limit, { targets, occurrences }));
        this.internals = {
            executor,
            service: new DefaultSchedulerService(store, occurrences, targets),
        };
        return this.internals;
    }

E20 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-scheduler/SKILL.md:49–49

“Do not modify schedule_definitions、schedule_occurrences … and do not add jobs under Scheduler's @nocobase/app-plugin-scheduler scope.”

Schedules are defined in code. There is no management API for creating or editing Cron definitions, but the UI and API support enabling and disabling tasks. Do not modify `schedule_definitions`, `schedule_occurrences`, or the schedule backend's rules (BullMQ job schedulers, or the memory adapter's state file) directly, and do not add jobs under Scheduler's `@nocobase/app-plugin-scheduler` scope.

E21 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-scheduler/references/operations.md:41–41

列表/发生记录/启停只通过 HTTP API，同步通过 CLI；store/registry/executor 私有，应用不能通过容器解析绕过 Routes 授权。

Reading and changing schedules — list, occurrences, enable and disable — is reachable through the HTTP API above, and synchronization through `pnpm nocobase scheduler sync`. Neither is exposed as a resolvable service: the store, the target registry and the schedule executor are private to the plugin, so an application cannot bypass the authorization its Routes enforce by resolving them from the container.

/**
 * The scope the plugin registers its executors under. Declared in the plugin's
 * provider but not publicly exported, so it is repeated here; it must match, or
 * `getScheduleExecutor` hands back a different executor with no handlers.
 */
const SCHEDULER_SCOPE = '@nocobase/app-plugin-scheduler';

E23 · app/server/service/schedule-runner.ts:144–187

应用把 schedulerServiceToken cast 为 ScheduleEventRecorder、解析 jobExecutorServiceToken.getScheduleExecutor 取私有 runner.run，并手写 ScheduleStart/Error/End 事件记录。

  const service = app.container.resolve(
    schedulerServiceToken,
  ) as unknown as ScheduleEventRecorder;
  // The plugin resolves its executor with this same configuration name, and the
  // jobs service caches by (configuration, scope); a different name would build
  // a second executor whose handler table has none of the plugin's schedules.
  const jobsName = app.config.get<string>('scheduler.jobs');
  const executor = app.container
    .resolve(jobExecutorServiceToken)
    .getScheduleExecutor(SCHEDULER_SCOPE, jobsName);
  const runner = (executor as unknown as ExecutorWithRunner).runner;
  if (!runner) {
    return undefined;
  }

  const scheduleId = String(definition.id);
  const now = new Date();
  const occurrenceId = `${scheduleId}:manual:${Date.now()}.${Math.random()
    .toString(36)
    .slice(2, 8)}`;
  // Keep the plan's next timed firing: the store writes `nextRunAt` from every
  // event it records, so omitting it here would clear the schedule's next run.
  const nextRunAt = dateValue(definition.nextRunAt);
  const base = {
    jobId: occurrenceId,
    jobName: scheduleId,
    scheduledAt: now,
    runAt: now,
    ...(nextRunAt ? { nextRunAt } : {}),
  };

  await service.recordEvent({ name: 'ScheduleStart', ...base });
  try {
    await runner.run({ ...base, signal: AbortSignal.timeout(120_000) });
  } catch (error) {
    await service.recordEvent({
      name: 'ScheduleError',
      ...base,
      reason: 'execute-failed',
      error: error instanceof Error ? error : new Error(String(error)),
    });
    throw error;
  }
  await service.recordEvent({ name: 'ScheduleEnd', ...base });

E27 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-notification/dist/server/manager.js:211–236

send(input) 实现幂等键校验、source 校验、messages Channel 校验，并禁止 to/channels/content/routing 等遗留属性。

    async send(input) {
        await this.activate();
        validateNotificationIdempotencyKey(input.idempotencyKey);
        if (input.source !== undefined &&
            (!input.source ||
                typeof input.source.type !== 'string' ||
                !input.source.type.trim() ||
                (input.source.referenceId !== undefined &&
                    typeof input.source.referenceId !== 'string')))
            throw new Error('Notification source requires a type and an optional string referenceId.');
        if (!input.messages ||
            typeof input.messages !== 'object' ||
            Array.isArray(input.messages))
            throw new Error('Notification messages must be a Channel-to-message map.');
        for (const key of [
            'to',
            'channels',
            'content',
            'routing',
            'channelOverrides',
        ])
            if (key in input)
                throw new Error(`Unsupported notification send property "${key}".`);
        const channels = Object.keys(input.messages);
        if (channels.length === 0)
            throw new Error('At least one notification message is required.');
